Title: India Orientalis Christiana continens fundationes ecclesiarum, seriem episcoporum, missiones, schismata, persecutiones, reges, viros illustres
Creator: Paulinus a S. Bartholomaeo, Discalced Carmelite (title page)
Publisher: Rome: Typis Salomonianis, 1794 (title page: 'ANNO DOM. MDCCXCIV')
Publication Date: 1794 (title page, read directly)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1794); the archive.org record carries the Public Domain Mark (licenseurl publicdomain/mark/1.0)
Language: lat
Source: archive.org item bub_gb_uwMQAAAAIAAJ, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-10 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (427,567) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1794, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: fair. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: A Carmelite missionary's history of the churches of India, including both obediences of the St Thomas Christians. At about OCR lines 5006-5016 he cites a Syriac letter of the sixth Mar Thoma (Mar Dionysius), copied from the Propaganda archive, and his own meeting with him in December 1785; Joseph Kariattil, 'indigena Malabarensis', at about 3485.

VICARII APOSTOLICI MALABARIAE . 

Supremum obiit diem D. Alexius Cenczcs ex Goa- 
no Archiepiscopo Hispaniarum Primas et Antistes 
Bracharensis anno \ 6 ij. die j.Maii(i). Eodem 
anno secundus ab illo Angamalensis Praesul Stc- 
phanus de Brito ecclesiam Malabarensem regendam 
susceperat. Vix ergo Mcnezius obitu suo oculos 
clauserat , jam illa infelix Angamalensis seu Cran- 
ganorensis, tot laboribus, tot sudoribus et impen- 
sis Mcnczii ad Catholicam fidem et Ecclesiae uni- 
tatem traducta Nestoriana dioecesis , malignantium 
sacerdotum agitata furoribus ad veterem toto co- 
natu redire tentavit insaniam . Extat apud me co- 
dex ms. R. P. Udephonsi a Praesentatione olim Ma- 
labariae Missionarii , qui cx tabulariis Missionis 
Verapolitanac epistolam profert Lusitano sermone 
scriptam , quam Archidiaconus Gcorgius et cum co
